Nsaba okusonyiyibwa gy’oli ku abo abatannakkiriza, abatannakwagala, abatannasuubira era abatannakwagala. Omutukuvu Katonda Omusatu, Kitaffe, Omwana ne Mwoyo Mutukuvu, nkukwatako nnyo, era nkuwa omubiri ogusinga obulungi, omusaayi, emmeeme n’obutukuvu bwa Yesu Kristo, ali mu masinzizo gonna ag’ensi, ng’asasula olw’okukola ebibi, okunyooma n’obutafaayo kwe yennyini akwatiddwa. Era, okuyita mu bulungi obutaggwaawo bw’Omutima gwe Omutukuvu ennyo, n’Omutima omutukuvu ogwa Maryamu, nkusaba okukyusa aboonoona abatalina kyekikulu. |
| Our Father, Hail Mary Glory Be (three times each) | Kitaffe, Mukama Mariya Ekitiibwa kibeere gyoli (emirundi esatu buli kimu) |
| Prayer to St. Dominic | Okusaba eri St. Dominic |
| O renowned champion of the faith of Christ, most holy saint Dominic, who didst renounce the honour and dignity of an earthly principality to embrace the poor, laborious, and mortified life which should distinguish a disciple of Him Who has said: If any man will come after Me, let him take up his cross and follow Me. | Omuvubuka omumanyiddwa ennyo mu kukkiriza kwa Kristu, omutukuvu Dominic omutukuvu ennyo, eyaleka ekitiibwa n’obugagga bw’ensi okusanyusa abavu, abakozi, era abawulirizibwa obulumi obw’okutambulira mu bulamu obulina okwawula omuyigirizwa we Oyo agamba nti: Omuntu yenna bw’aba ayagala okunkolera emabega, amuteekeko omusalaba gwe angoberere. |
| O burning torch, who being thyself consumed with the fire of divine love, didst incessantly labour to enkindle that sacred flame in the hearts of others, look down upon me from that throne of glory where thou enjoyest the reward of all thy labours, and obtain that some sparks of that blessed fire may be lighted in my soul, to animate and encourage me under any crosses or trials with which it shall please the divine goodness to visit me. | O torch eyaka, ggwe nga wekka okyayokebwa omuliro gw’okwagala kwa Katonda, wakola bulijjo okukuma omuliro ogutukuvu mu mitima gy’abalala, nnyonera wansi okuva ku kiti ky’obugagga gy’ofunira empeera y’emirimu gyo gyonna, onfunireko obusannyalazo bw’omuliro ogwo ogw’ekisa bubeere mu mwoyo gwange, okunyweza n’okunsanyusa mu buvunaanyizibwa oba ebigezo byonna Katonda by’anaakiriza okunkuba. |
| Thou, O great Saint, regardest as nothing all the afflictions thou didst endure, and all the toils thou didst undergo for the promotion of God’s holy cause; obtain, I beseech Thee, that the same ardent love which strengthened thee, may make sweet to me labours, humiliations, disgraces, or whatever other mortifications I may have to suffer for the Name of Jesus. | Gwe, Omutukuvu omukulu, tolowooza ku buzibu bwonna bwe wawulira oba emirimu gyonna gye wakola okutumbula ekigendererwa kya Katonda ekitukuvu; nkwegayiridde, nfuna okwagala okwo okw’amaanyi okwakusobozesa, kunnyumire emirimu, okuswazibwa, okuswaza, oba ebintu ebirala byonna bye nnaasobola okufiirwa olw’erinnya lya Yesu. |
| May I ever bear in mind that nothing can happen to me but by the particular dispensation of a God Who is infinitely wise, and therefore knows what is best for my welfare; infinitely powerful, and consequently able to effect it; and above all, infinitely merciful and loving, Who has laid down His life for my redemption, and continues daily to give new proofs of His love and bounty. | Nnyinza okujjukira nti tewali kintu kyonna kisobola kunzizaako wabula nga Katonda ow’amagezi agatagambika, era amanyi ekisinga obulungi eri obulamu bwange; ow’amaanyi agatagambika, era asobola okukikola; ate okusinga byonna, ow’ekisa ekitagambika n’okwagala, eyawaayo obulamu bwe ku lwange, era akiraga buli lunaku mu kwagala kwe n’obugabi bwe. |
| For a Spirit of Compassion | Okuba n'omwoyo gw'okusaasira |
| O tender Father of the poor who, when all other resources were exhausted, didst offer thyself for their relief, obtain for me that true spirit of compassion for the suffering members of Jesus Christ, which shone forth so conspicuously in thy holy life. | Omutonzi wa taata w'abavu, eyawaddeyo obulamu bwe okubayamba nga ebintu byonna bikendedde, nkusaba onnyambe nfune omwoyo ogw'obusaasizi eri abo abali mu bulumi mu Yesu Kristo, ogwalaga mu bulamu bwo obutukuvu. |
| May I, by charity to those whom Jesus so dearly loves, lay up for myself treasures in heaven, where thou now enjoyest that which the eye hath not seen, nor the ear heard, nor the heart of man conceived, but which God has prepared for those who love Him, and who prove themselves His Disciples by the observance of His Divine precept: love one another. | Nnyinza, nga nkozesa obulungi bwange eri abo Yesu b’ayagala ennyo, okuteekateeka eby’obugagga mu ggulu, gy’oli kati weewummulira ekyo amaaso gatakirabye, wadde okutu kwawulidde, wadde omutima gw’omuntu gwakitegedde, naye Katonda ky’ataddewo eri abamwagala era abakakasa nga bayizi be nga bakwata amateeka ge ag’obutukuvu: mwagalane. |
| I praise and thank God for the high degree of sanctity to which He had raised thee, and the special privileges by which He has distinguished thee. | Ntendereza era neebaza Katonda olw'obutukuvu obungi bwe yakuwa, n'enkizo ez'enjawulo z'akugabira. |
| I ask thee, by that gratitude with which thou shalt for all eternity be penetrated for thy Divine Benefactor, implore for me the grace to root out of my heart whatever is not agreeable in His sight, especially that evil habit by which I most frequently offend Him. | Nkwebuuza, olw'okusiima kwe onookozesa okumala emirembe gyonna okukusanyusa Omutonzi wo ow'ekitalo, nsaba ompe ekisa okuggya mu mutima gwange buli kimu kyonna ekitali kirungi mu maaso ge, naddala empisa embi gye nkola ennyo nga mmunyiiza. |
| Obtain likewise the favours I request in this Novena, through thy powerful intercession. | Funa mu ngeri y'emu ebirungi bye nsaba mu Novena eno, okuyitira mu kusabira kwo okw'amaanyi. |
| To Our Lady, Queen of the Rosary | Eri Mukama waffe, Kwiini w'Essaala ya Rozali |
| O glorious Mother of God, Queen of the most sacred Rosary, thou who didst love Dominic with the affection of a mother, and were most tenderly loved and honoured by him, look upon me, for his sake, with an eye of pity, deign to join with him in presenting these petitions to thy most Blessed Jesus. | O Mukama w'ekitiibwa, Maama wa Katonda, Kabaka w'Omusaalaba ogutukuvu ennyo, ggwe eyayagala Dominic ng'omukwano gw'omuzadde, era n'okwagalwa n'okuweebwa ekitiibwa mu maaso ge, ndabirira, ku lw'obulungi bwe, n'amaaso ag'ekisa, okwegatta naye mu kuleeta okusaba kuno eri Yesu wo omutukuvu. |
| I sincerely desire from this moment to love Him with all my heart, and serve Him with all my strength, and now place myself under thy powerful protection, as a sure means of obtaining all the graces necessary to serve Him faithfully here, that I may eternally rejoice with Him hereafter. Amen. | Nnyinza okwagala okuva kati okumwagala n'omutima gwange gwonna, era okumuweereza n'amaanyi gange gonna, era kati nteeka obulamu bwange mu bukuumi bwo obw'amaanyi, nga kino kyekyokukozesa okufuna ebirungi byonna ebyetaagisa okumuweereza mu bwesigwa wano, nsobole okusanyuka naye emirembe gyonna. Amiina. |
| Prayer to Our Lady of Sorrows | Okusaba eri Omukyala waffe ow'Enaku |
| O sorrowful Mother, I turn to you in total trust. You suffered the sharpest pains in life, watching your Son die upon the Cross, and yet you remained by Him to the end. | O Maama ow'ennaku, nkwesigamako mu bwesige obujjuvu. Wabonaabona ennyo mu bulamu, ng'olaba Omwana wo afa ku Musalaba, era wabangawo okutuuka ku nkomerero. |
| Look with favor upon me, a poor sinner, and obtain for me from your Son all the graces I need to endure the sufferings God allows me to face. | Nnyonera ekisa, omwonoonyi omwavu, era onfunire okuva mu Mwana wo ebirungi byonna bye nneetaaga okugumira okubonaabona Katonda kw'ankkiriza okwolekagana nakwo. |
| At the Cross Her Station Keeping | Ku Kkubo lye ery'Okusala |
| On the Cross her Son was dying. Mary stood beneath Him crying, Sharing in His saving cross. As He hangs, her soul is grieving, and a sword her heart is cleaving and she weeps the bitter loss. | Ku musalaba, Omwana we yali afiira. Malyamu yayimirira wansi We ng'akaaba, Ng'ayita mu musalaba ogw'okulokola kwe. Bwe yali agudde, emmeeme ye yali ewulira ennaku, Era ekitala kyakuba omutima gwe nga kimusala, N'akaaba olw'okufiirwa okunakuwaza. |
| The First Sorrow | Okunakuwala Okusooka |
| O Mother of Sorrows, through thy First Sorrow, the Prophecy of Holy Simeon, intercede for me with the Sacred Heart of Jesus, and grant me the favor I implore (mention request here…) | O Maama w'Okunakuwala, okuyita mu Kunakuwala kwo Okusooka, Obunnabbi bwa Simeoni Omutukuvu, onkwatireko n'Omutima omutukuvu gwa Yesu, era ompa ekisa kye nsaba (teekamu okusaba kwo wano…) |
| The Memorare | Memorare |
| Remember, O most gracious Virgin Mary, that never was it known that anyone who fled to your protection, implored your help or sought your intercession, was left unaided. Inspired by this confidence, I fly unto you, O Virgin of Virgins, my Mother. To you do I come, before you I stand, sinful and sorrowful. | Jjukira, O Mawulire Omugole Maryamu, nti tewali muntu yenna eyadduka okufuna obukuumi bwo, n’asaba obuyambi bwo oba n’anoonya okusabirwa kwo, eyalekebwa nga talina buyambi. Nga nsikiriziddwa ku bwesige buno, nkuleetera eri, O Mugole w’Abagole, Maama wange. Nkujja gyoli, mu maaso go nnyimiridde, nga ndi munyoomi era omunakuwavu. |
| O Mother of the Word Incarnate, despise not my petitions, but in your clemency, hear and answer me. Amen. | O Maama w'Ekigambo Ekifuuse Omuntu, tokolokota kusaba kwange, naye mu kisa kyo, mpuliriza era onziramu. Amiina. |
| Prayers to Our Lady of Guadalupe | Okusaba eri Mukama waffe ow'e Guadalupe |
| Patroness of the Americas | Omukulembeze w'Amawanga ga America |
| First Prayer | Essaala esooka |
| Our Lady Of Guadalupe, mystical rose, make intercession for the Holy Church; protect the Holy Father; help all those who invoke you in their needs. | Mukama waffe ow'e Guadalupe, omulanga ogw'ekyewuunyo, okwegayirira ku lwa Eklezia Entukuvu; okukuuma Kitaawe Omutukuvu; okuyamba bonna abakusaba mu byetaago byabwe. |
| Since you are the ever Virgin Mary and Mother of the true God, obtain for us from your most holy Son Jesus the grace of keeping our faith, sweet hope in the midst of the difficulties of life, burning charity, and the precious gift of final perseverance. Amen. | Okuva bw'oli omukazi Omweru Maria era Maama w'Katonda ow'amazima, tuwe okuva eri Omwana wo omutukuvu Yesu ekisa eky'okukuuma okukkiriza kwaffe, essuubi ery'omuwendo mu buzibu obw'obulamu, okwagala okungi, n'ekirabo eky'omuwendo eky'okugumiikiriza. Amiina. |
| Second Prayer | Essaala ey'okubiri |
| O most gracious Virgin of Guadalupe, in your apparitions on Mount Tepeyac, you promised to show pity and compassion to all who, loving and trusting you, seek your help and protection. | Omutukuvu Omubereberye ow'e Guadalupe, mu kulabikako kwo ku Lusozi Tepeyac, wasuubiza okulaga obusaasizi n'obulungi eri abo bonna abakwagala era abakwesiga, nga banoonya obuyambi n'obukuumi bwo. |
| Listen to our supplications and grant us consolation and relief. We are full of hope that, relying on your help, nothing can trouble or affect us. As you have remained with us through your admirable image, so now obtain for us the graces we need. Amen. | Wulira okusaba kwaffe era otuwa obuwumbi n’okutereeza. Tuli n’essuubi nti, nga twesigama ku buyambi bwo, tewali kintu kisobola kutweraliikiriza oba okutukosa. Nga bw’oba ng’otaddeko amaanyi mu kifaananyi ky’oyagala, kaakati tuwe ebyetaago byaffe. Amiina. |
| Our Lady of Fatima Novena Prayer | Essaala ya Our Lady of Fatima Novena |
| Our Lady of the Rosary of Fatima | Mukama waffe ow'ekisumuluzo e Fatima |
| Novena Prayer | Okusaba kwa Novena |
| Most Holy Virgin, who deigned to come to Fatima, to reveal the treasures of grace hidden in the recitation of the Rosary, inspire our hearts with a sincere love of this devotion, that meditating on the Mysteries of our Redemption recalled therein, we may obtain the conversion of sinners, the conversion of Russia, and (here name the other favors you are praying for), which we ask of you in this Novena, for the greater glory of God, for your own honor, and for the good of souls. Amen. | Omutukuvu Omulongo, eyakkiriza okujja e Fatima, okulaga obugagga bw’ekisa ekisibiddwa mu kusoma Rosary, atusabe omutima ogw’okwagalira ddala okwekkiriza kuno, nga tusaba ku Mysteries of Redemption, tusobole okufuna okukyusa aboonoonyi, okukyusa Russia, n’ebirala byonna bye musaba, olw’ekitiibwa kya Katonda, ekitiibwa kyammwe, n’obulungi bw’emyoyo. Amen. |
| Our Lady of the Rosary of Fatima, Pray for us. | Mukyala waffe ow'ekikomo e Fatima, Tusabire. |
| The Canticle of Mary (Luke 1:46-55) | Oluyimba lwa Maliyamu (Lukka 1:46-55) |
| The Canticle of Mary | Oluyimba lwa Mariya |
| My soul proclaims the greatness of the Lord, my spirit rejoices in God, my savior, for he has looked with favor on his lowly servant. From this day all generations will call me blessed: the Almighty has done great things for me, and holy is his Name. | Omwoyo gwange gutendereza obukulu bwa Mukama, omutima gwange gusanyuka mu Katonda omulokozi wange, kubanga atunuulidde omuweereza we ow’obwetoowaze n’ekisa. Okuva ku lunaku luno emirembe gyonna girinnyonnyola nga ndi wa mukisa: Omutonzi Omuyinza yakoze ebintu ebinene ku lwange, era erinnya lye litukuvu. |
| He has shown mercy on those who fear him in every generation. | Alaze ekisa eri abo bonna abamutya mu buli mulembe. |
| He has shown the strength of his arm, he has scattered the proud in their conceit. | Alaze amaanyi g'omukono gwe, asansudde ab'amalala mu kwebuulirira kwabwe. |
| He has cast down the mighty from their thrones, and has lifted up the lowly. | Asse ab'amaanyi okuva ku ntebe zaabwe, era ayimusa abaavu. |
| He has filled the hungry with good things, and the rich he has sent away empty. | Ajjuza abalumwa enjala ebirungi, n'abagagga abasindika nga tebalina kantu. |
| He has come to the help of his servant Israel for he has remembered his promise of mercy, the promise he made to our fathers, to Abraham and his children forever. | Azze okuyamba omuweereza we Isiraeri kubanga ajjukidde ekisuubizo kye eky'obusaasizi, ekisuubizo kye yakola bajjajja baffe, Aburahamu n'abaana be emirembe gyonna. |
| Scripture Reference | Ebyawandiikibwa ebijuliziddwamu |
| Luke 1:46-55 | Lukka 1:46-55 |
| "The Lord is my shepherd; I shall not want. He makes me lie down in green pastures." | "Mukama ye musumba wange; sirina kye nneetaaga. Anzisa mu kisibo ekirungi." |
| Psalm 23:1–2 | Zabbuli 23:1–2 |
| "Let all that you do be done in love." | Byonna by'okola bikolebwa mu kwagala |
| 1 Corinthians 16:14 | 1 Abakkolinso 16:14 |
| Apostles' Creed | Okukkiriza kw'Abatume |
| I believe in God, the Father Almighty, Creator of Heaven and earth; and in Jesus Christ, His only Son, Our Lord, Who was conceived by the Holy Spirit, born of the Virgin Mary, suffered under Pontius Pilate, was crucified, died, and was buried. He descended into Hell; the third day He rose again from the dead; He ascended into Heaven, and sits at the right hand of God, the Father Almighty; from thence He shall come to judge the living and the dead. I believe in the Holy Spirit, the holy Catholic Church, the communion of saints, the forgiveness of sins, the resurrection of the body, and life everlasting. Amen. | Nzikiriza mu Katonda, Kitaawe Omuyinza w’ebintu byonna, Omutonzi w’eggulu n’ensi; ne mu Yesu Kristo, Omwana we omu yekka, Mukama waffe, eyazaalibwa Omwoyo Omutukuvu, era n’azaalibwa Namalaaya Maria, n’abonyaabonyezebwa wansi wa Pontiyo Piraato, n’akomererwa ku musaalaba, n’afa, n’aziikibwa. Yagenda mu hell; ku lunaku olwokusatu n’azuukira okuva mu bafu; yalinnya mu ggulu, era atuula ku mukono ogwa ddyo ogwa Katonda, Kitaawe Omuyinza w’ebintu byonna; okuva eyo ajja kujja okusalira abalamu n’abafu omusango. Nzikiriza mu Mwoyo Omutukuvu, Ekkanisa Enkatolika entukuvu, obumu bw’abatukuvu, okusonyiyibwa kw’ebibi, okuzuukira kw’omubiri, n’obulamu obutaggwaawo. Amiina. |
| Our Father | Kitaffe |
| Our Father, Who art in heaven, hallowed be Thy name; Thy kingdom come; Thy will be done on earth as it is in heaven. Give us this day our daily bread; and forgive us our trespasses as we forgive those who trespass against us; and lead us not into temptation, but deliver us from evil. Amen. | Kitaffe ali mu ggulu, erinnya lyo litukuzibwe; Obwakabaka bwo bujje; Ebyo by’oyagala bikolebwe ku nsi nga bwe bikolebwa mu ggulu. Tuwe leero emmere yaffe eya buli lunaku; era otusonyiwe ebyonoono byaffe nga ffe bwe tusonyiwa abatusobya; era otutwale mu kugezesebwa, wabula otuwonye okuva mu bibi. Amiina. |
| Hail Mary | Hail Mary |
| Hail Mary, full of grace, the Lord is with thee. Blessed art thou among women, and blessed is the fruit of thy womb, Jesus. Holy Mary, Mother of God, pray for us sinners, now and at the hour of our death. Amen. | Hail Mary, ojjudde ekisa, Mukama ali naawe. Oweereddwa omukisa mu bakazi bonna, era n'ekibala ky'olubuto lwo, Yesu, kye kirina omukisa. Maria omutukuvu, Maama wa Katonda, tusabire ffe aboonoonyi, kati ne ku ssaawa y'okufa kwaffe. Amen. |
| Glory Be | Ekitiibwa kibeere |
| Glory be to the Father, and to the Son, and to the Holy Spirit. As it was in the beginning, is now, and ever shall be, world without end. Amen. | Ekitiibwa kibeere eri Kitaffe, n’eri Omwana, n’eri Omwoyo Omutukuvu. Nga bwe kyali mu ntandikwa, bwe kiri kaakati, era kijja kubeera bulijjo, ensi etalina kkomo. Amiina. |
| Fatima Prayer | Fatima Prayer |
| O my Jesus, forgive us our sins, save us from the fires of hell, lead all souls to Heaven, especially those in most need of Thy mercy. Amen. | O Yesu wange, tusonyiwe ebibi byaffe, otuwonye okuva mu muliro gw'omuliro ogw'omu hell, omutwale emyoyo gyonna mu ggulu, naddala abo abali mu bwetaavu obusinga obungi obw'obusaasizi bwo. Amiina. |
| Hail Holy Queen | Hail Queen omutukuvu |
| Hail, Holy Queen, Mother of Mercy, our life, our sweetness and our hope! To thee do we cry, poor banished children of Eve; to thee do we send up our sighs, mourning and weeping in this valley of tears. Turn then, most gracious advocate, thine eyes of mercy toward us, and after this our exile, show unto us the blessed fruit of thy womb, Jesus. O clement, O loving, O sweet Virgin Mary! Pray for us, O Holy Mother of God, that we may be made worthy of the promises of Christ. Amen. | Hail, Omukyala omutukuvu, Maama w’obusaasizi, obulamu bwaffe, essanyu lyaffe n’essuubi lyaffe! Gwe gwe tukubira, abaana abavuunika ba Eve; gwe gwe tusindika okukaaba kwaffe, nga tukungubaga era nga tukuba amaziga mu kikko kino eky’amaziga. Kyusa amaaso go ag’obusaasizi otutunuulire, era oluvannyuma lw’okugobebwa kuno, tulage ekibala ekirungi ekiva mu lubuto lwo, Yesu. O clement, O mwagalwa, O Virgin Mary omulungi! Tusabire, Maama omutukuvu wa Katonda, tuleme okufuna ebisuubizo bya Kristo. Amen. |
| Daily Offering | Okuwaayo buli lunaku |
| O Jesus, through the Immaculate Heart of Mary, I offer you my prayers, works, joys and sufferings of this day, for all the intentions of your Sacred Heart, in union with the Holy Sacrifice of the Mass throughout the world, in reparation for sin, for the intentions of all my friends and associates, and in particular for the intentions of the Holy Father. | O Yesu, okuyita mu Mutima Omutukuvu gwa Maria, nkutendereza n’okusaba kwange, emirimu gyange, essanyu lyange n’okubonaabona kwange olwaleero, ku lw’ebigendererwa by’Omutima Gwo Omutukuvu, nga nkolera wamu n’Ekirabo ekitukuvu ekya Misa mu nsi yonna, ng’ekyokuddamu eri ebibi, ku lw’ebigendererwa by’abagenyi bange bonna n’abalala, era naddala ku lw’ebigendererwa bya Kitaawe Omutukuvu. |
| My God, I believe in you and all that you have taught, because you have said it, and your word is true. | Katonda wange, nzikiriza mu ggwe ne byonna bye wayigiriza kubanga obimbyeera n'ekigambo kyo kituufu. |
| I hope in your promise of Eternal life and I ask for your mercy and strength this day. | Nsuubira mu kisuubizo kyo eky'obulamu obutaggwaawo era nkusaba okusaasira n'amaanyi go ku lunaku luno. |
| Act of Charity | Ekikolwa ky'Obuyambi |
| O My God, I love you with my whole heart and above all things, because you are infinitely good and perfect; and I love my neighbours as myself for love of you. Grant that I may love you more and more in this life, and in the next for all eternity. | O Katonda wange, nkukwatako n'omutima gwange gwonna era okusinga byonna kubanga oli mulungi nnyo era mutuufu; era njagala baliraanwa bange nga bwe njagala nze olw'okukwagala. Nkwegayirira onnyambe nkulekere okukwagala ennyo mu bulamu buno n'obulamu obuliddako emirembe gyonna. |
| Morning Consecration Prayers | Okusaba okw'okutongoza ku makya |
| Merciful Jesus, I consecrate myself today and always to Your Most Sacred Heart. | Yesu ow'ekisa, nneewaayo gy'oli leero era bulijjo eri omutima gwo ogutukuvu ennyo. |
| Most Sacred Heart of Jesus I implore, that I may ever love you more and more. | Omutima Omutukuvu ogwa Yesu nkwegayirira, nnyongere okwagala ennyo. |
| Most Sacred Heart of Jesus, I trust in you! | Omutima Omutukuvu ennyo ogwa Yesu, nkakasa mu ggwe! |
| Most Sacred Heart of Jesus, have mercy on us! | Omutima Omutukuvu ennyo ogwa Yesu, tulage ekisa! |
| Sacred Heart of Jesus, I believe in your love for me. | Omutima omutukuvu ogwa Yesu, nzikiriza mu kwagala kwo eri nze. |
| Jesus, meek and humble of heart, make my heart like your heart. | Yesu, omuwombeefu era ow'omutima omugonvu, ffeesa omutima gwange okuba ng'ogwo. |
| Prayer of Thanksgiving (Night) | Okusaba okw'okwebaza (ekiro) |
| O My God, I thank you for all the benefits which I have received from you this day. Give me light to see what sins I have committed, and grant me grace to be truly sorry for them. | O Katonda wange, nkwebaza olw'emiganyulo gyonna gye nfuna okuva gyoli leero. Mpa ekitangaala okulaba ebibi bye nnakola, era onnyambe nsonyiwe ddala mu mazima. |
| Act of Contrition (Night) | Ekikolwa ky'Okwenenya (Ekiro) |
| O my God, because you are so good, I am very sorry that I have sinned against you. With the help of your grace I will try not to sin again. | O Katonda wange, kubanga oli mulungi nnyo, nsaba nnyo nti nnayonoona gy’oli. N’obuyambi bw’ekisa kyo, ndigenda okugezaako obutaddamu kukola bubi. |
| All Praise to You | Ekitiibwa kyonna kibeere gy'oli |
| All praise to you, O God, this night for the blessings of the light. Keep us, we pray, O King of kings, Beneath your own almighty wings. | Ekitiibwa kyonna kibeere gy'oli, Ai Katonda, ekiro kino olw'emikisa gy'ekitangaala. Tukuume, twegayirira, Ai Kabaka w'abakabaka, Wansi w'amagulu go ag'amaanyi ennyo. |
| Forgive us, Lord, through Christ Your Son, Whatever wrong this day we've done. Your peace give to the world, O Lord, That we might live in one accord. | Tusonyiwe, Mukama, okuyita mu Kristo Omwana wo, Ekibi kyonna kye tukola olunaku luno. Emirembe gyo gituuse ku nsi, O Mukama, Tusobole okubeera wamu mu bumu. |
| Enlighten us, O blessed light, And give us rest throughout this night. O strengthen us, that for your sake, We all may serve you when we wake. | Tuyigirize, ggwe ekitangaala ekisanyusa, Otuleete okuwummula mu kiro kino kyonna. Otunyweze, olw'ekigendererwa kyo, Ffenna tusobole okukukolera nga tuvaamu. |
| The Angel of the Lord declared to Mary: And she conceived of the Holy Spirit. Hail Mary... | Malayika wa Mukama yagamba Malyamu nti: Era n'afuna olubuto lwa Mwoyo Omutukuvu. Mwana Maria... |
| Behold the handmaid of the Lord: Be it done to me according to your word. Hail Mary... | Laba omuweereza wa Mukama: Kikolebwe gye ndi nga bw'oyogedde. Weebale Mary... |
| And the Word was made flesh: And dwelt among us. Hail Mary... | Era Ekigambo kyafuuka omubiri: N'abeera mu ffe. Hail Mary... |
| Pray for us, O holy Mother of God. That we may be made worthy of the promise of Christ. | Tusabire, O Mukama Omukazi omutukuvu wa Katonda. Tufune obusobozi okutuukiriza ekisuubizo kya Kristo. |
| Let us pray: Pour forth, we beseech you, O Lord, your grace into our hearts that we, to whom the incarnation of Christ, your Son, was made known by the message of an angel, may by His passion and cross be brought to the glory of His resurrection, through the same Christ our Lord. Amen. | Ka tusabe: Tusaba, Mukama waffe, otuwe ekisa kyo mu mitima gyaffe, ffe abamanyi okufuuka kwa Kristo, Omwana wo, nga kyategeezebwa mu bubaka bw’omumalaika, tuyitibwe ku kufa kwe n’omusalaba gwe okutuuka ku kitiibwa ky’okuzuukira kwe, okuyita mu Kristo omwami waffe. Amiina. |
| O God, by the resurrection of Your Son, our Lord Jesus Christ, You have willed to make glad the whole world. Grant, we beseech You, that through His Mother, the Virgin Mary, we may lay hold of the joys of eternal life. We ask this through the same Christ our Lord. Amen. | Ai Katonda, olw’okuzuukira kwa Mwana wo, Mukama waffe Yesu Kristo, oyagala okusanyusa ensi yonna. Tukusaba otuwe obulamu obutaggwaawo nga tuyita mu Maama we, Omukyala Maria. Kino tukisaba mu Kristo omu oyo Mukama waffe. Amiina. |
| Divine Mercy Chaplet (3 O'Clock Prayer) | Essaala ya Divine Mercy Chaplet (Essaala ey'essaawa 3) |
| You expired Jesus, but the source of life gushed forth for souls, and the ocean of mercy opened up for the whole world. O Fount of Life, unfathomable Divine Mercy, envelop the whole world and empty Yourself out upon us. | Waweddeko Yesu, naye ensibuko y’obulamu yateekawo obulamu eri emyoyo, era ennyanja y’obusaasizi n’eggulirwa ensi yonna. O Fount of Life, Obusaasizi bwa Katonda obutalina kiggwa, buzinga ensi yonna era weeyanjule ku ffe. |
| O Blood and Water which gushed forth from the Heart of Jesus as a fount of mercy for us, I trust in you! | Omusaayi n'amazzi ebyava mu mutima gwa Yesu ng'ensulo y'ekisa eri ffe, nkakasa nti ggwe njagala! |
| Holy God, Holy Mighty One, Holy Immortal One, Have Mercy on us and on the whole world. (Say 3 times) Amen. | Katonda omutukuvu, Omuyinza omukulu, Oyo atafa, Tusaasire n'ensi yonna. (Kigambe emirundi esatu) Amiina. |
| Leader: Our Father... All together: Give us this day... Leader: Hail Mary... All together: Holy Mary... | Omukulembeze: Kitaffe... Ffenna awamu: Tuwe ku lunaku luno... Omukulembeze: Hail Mary... Ffenna awamu: Malyamu omutukuvu... |
| All together pray the Creed: I believe in God, the Father almighty, the creator of heaven and earth and in Jesus Christ, His only Son, Our Lord, who was conceived by the Holy Spirit, born of the Virgin Mary, suffered under Pontius Pilate, was crucified, died and was buried. He descended into hell. On the third day He rose again from the dead. He ascended into heaven and sits at the right hand of God, the Father almighty. From thence He shall come to judge the living and the dead. I believe in the Holy Spirit, the holy Catholic Church, the Communion of Saints, the forgiveness of sins, the resurrection of the body and life everlasting. Amen. | Mwenna musabire wamu Creed: Nzikiriza mu Katonda, Kitaffe omuyinza byonna, omutonzi w’eggulu n’ensi ne mu Yesu Kristo, Omwana we yekka, Mukama waffe, eyazaalibwa Omwoyo Omutukuvu, n’azaalibwa Mariya Omugole, n’abonyaabonyezebwa wansi wa Pontius Pilate, n’akomererwa ku musaalaba, n’afa era n’aziikibwa. Yagendera mu hell. Ku lunaku olusatu yazukira okuva mu bafu. Yalinnya mu ggulu era atudde ku mukono ogwa ddyo ogwa Katonda, Kitaffe omuyinza byonna. Okuva eyo ajja kujja okulamula abalamu n’abafu. Nzikiriza mu Mwoyo Omutukuvu, Ekkanisa Enkatolika entukuvu, Obumu bw’Abatukuvu, okusonyiyibwa ebibi, okuzuukira kw’omubiri n’obulamu obutaggwaawo. Amiina. |
| (On the large bead before each decade) All together: Eternal Father, I offer you the Body and Blood, Soul and Divinity of Your dearly beloved Son, Our Lord Jesus Christ in atonement for our sins and those of the whole world. | (Okusinziira ku kintu ekikulu nga buli mwaka) Bonna awamu: Kitaffe ow’obulamu obutaggwaawo, nkuleetera omubiri n’omusaayi, emmeeme n’obwakatonda bw’Omwana wo gw’oyagala ennyo, Mukama waffe Yesu Kristo okusonyiyibwa ebibi byaffe n’eby’ensi yonna. |
| (On the ten small beads of each decade) Leader: For the sake of His sorrowful passion All together: Have mercy on us and on the whole world. | (Ku buli kagwa akatono mu kkumi) Omukulembeze: Ku lw'okulumwa kw'Omutonzi waffe Ffenna awamu: Otusaasire n'ensi yonna. |
| (Conclude with): Holy God, Holy Mighty One, Holy Immortal One, Have mercy on us and on the whole world. (say three times) Amen. Jesus, King of Mercy, I trust in you! (say 3 times) | (Ggweko nti): Katonda omutukuvu, Omuyinza ow’amaanyi, Oyo atafa, Tusaasire ffe n’ensi yonna. (kigambe emirundi esatu) Amiina. Yesu, Kabaka w’Obusaasizi, nkakasa mu ggwe! (kigambe emirundi esatu) |
| Note: The first line appears twice as it is to be read twice. | Weetegereze: Olunyiriri olusooka lulabika emirundi ebiri kubanga luteekeddwa okusomebwa emirundi ebiri. |
| Say 13 Our Father's and 13 Hail Mary's for 13 consecutive days. Publish this prayer, share it with a friend and give a Mass of Thanksgiving. | Saba Kitaawe waffe emirundi 13 ne Hail Mary emirundi 13 mu nnaku 13 eziddiriŋŋana. Okuba essaala eno, ogigabane n'omuntu omulala era okole Mmisa ey'okwebaza. |
| Our Lady of Guadalupe, mystical rose, make intercession for the Holy Church; protect the Holy Father; help all those who invoke you in their needs. | Mukama waffe ow'e Guadalupe, omulandira ogw'ekyama, okusabiriza Eklezia Entukuvu; okukuuma Kitaawe Omutukuvu; okuyamba abo bonna abakukoowoola mu byetaago byabwe. |
| O most gracious Virgin of Guadalupe, in your apparitions on Mount Tepeyac, you promised to show pity and compassion to all who, loving and trusting you, seek your help and protection. Listen to our supplications and grant us consolation and relief. We are full of hope that, relying on your help, nothing can trouble or affect us. As you have remained with us through your admirable image, so now obtain for us the graces we need. Amen. | Omutukuvu Virgin wa Guadalupe, mu kulabikako kwo ku lusozi Tepeyac, wateekawo okutegeeza nti ojja kuba n’obusaasizi n’obulungi eri abo bonna abakwagala era abakwesiga nga banoonya obuyambi n’okukuuma kwo. Wuliriza okusaba kwaffe era otuwe obuwanguzi n’okusanyuka. Tulina essuubi nti, nga twesigamye ku buyambi bwo, tewali kintu kyonna kisobola kututabangula oba okutukosa. Nga bw’osigadde naffe mu kifaananyi kyo ekirungi, ka tufune obulungi bwe twetaaga. Amiina. |
| Most Holy Virgin, who deigned to come to Fatima, to reveal the treasures of grace hidden in the recitation of the Rosary, inspire our hearts with a sincere love of this devotion, that meditating on the Mysteries of our Redemption recalled therein, we may obtain the conversion of sinners, the conversion of Russia, and (here name the other favors you are praying for) which we ask of you in this Novena, for the greater glory of God, for your own honor, and for the good of souls. Amen. | Omukyala Omutukuvu, eyakkiriza okujja e Fatima okulaga obugagga bw’ekisa ekisirikiddwa mu kusoma Rosary, atuteemu amaanyi n’okwagala okwenkanya ku kweyongera kw’okusaba kuno, nga okusoma ku byama by’Okununulibwa kwaffe bwe kutuyamba okufuna okukyusa aboonoonyi, okukyusa Russia, era (wano yogerako ebirala by’osabira) bye tusaba gy’oli mu Novena eno, olw’ekitiibwa kya Katonda, olw’ekitiibwa kyo, n’olw’obulungi bw’emyoyo. Amiina. |
| Our Lady of the Rosary of Fatima, pray for us. | Mukama waffe ow'ekisabiro eky'omusala e Fatima, tusabire. |
| My soul proclaims the greatness of the Lord, my spirit rejoices in God, my savior, for he has looked with favor on his lowly servant. From this day all generations will call me blessed: the Almighty has done great things for me, and holy is his Name. | Omwoyo gwange gutendereza obukulu bwa Mukama, omutima gwange gusanyuka mu Katonda, omulokozi wange, kubanga atunuulidde omuweereza we ow’obwetoowaze n’ekisa. Okuva ku lunaku luno emirembe gyonna girinnyonnyola nga ndi wa mukisa: Omutonzi Omuyinza byonna ankoleddewo ebikulu, era erinnya lye litukuvu. |
| He has shown mercy on those who fear him in every generation. | Alaze ekisa eri abo bonna abamutya mu buli mulembe. |
| He has shown the strength of his arm, he has scattered the proud in their conceit. | Alaze amaanyi g'omukono gwe, asaasaanyizza ab'amalala mu kwegulumiza kwabwe. |
| He has cast down the mighty from their thrones, and has lifted up the lowly. | Agobye ab'amaanyi ku ntebe zaabwe, n'agulumiza abaavu. |
| He has filled the hungry with good things, and the rich he has sent away empty. | Yajjuza abalumwa enjala ebirungi, era omugagga yamuwummuza nga talina kintu. |
| He has come to the help of his servant Israel for he has remembered his promise of mercy, the promise he made to our fathers, to Abraham and his children forever. | Azze okuyamba omuddu we Isirayiri kubanga ajjukidde ekisuubizo kye eky’obusaasizi, ekisuubizo kye yakola bajjajja baffe, eri Ibulayimu n’abaana be emirembe gyonna. |
| (Luke 1:46-55) | (Lukka 1:46-55) |
| Litany of the Blessed Virgin Mary | Ekitabo ky'omukyala omulongoofu Maria |
| Lord, have mercy. Lord, have mercy. Christ, have mercy. Christ, have mercy. Lord, have mercy. Lord, have mercy. Christ, hear us. Christ, graciously hear us. | Mukama, onyweze ekisa. Mukama, onyweze ekisa. Kristo, onyweze ekisa. Kristo, onyweze ekisa. Mukama, onyweze ekisa. Mukama, onyweze ekisa. Kristo, tuwulire. Kristo, tuwulire mu kisa. |
| God the Father of Heaven, have mercy on us. God the Son, Redeemer of the world, have mercy on us. God the Holy Spirit, have mercy on us. Holy Trinity, one God, have mercy on us. | Katonda Kitaffe ow'omu ggulu, twasaasire. Katonda Omwana, Omununuzi w'ensi, twasaasire. Katonda Omwoyo Omutukuvu, twasaasire. Tiriniti Entukuvu, Katonda omu, twasaasire. |
| Holy Mary, pray for us. Holy Mother of God, pray for us. Holy Virgin of virgins, pray for us. Mother of Christ, pray for us. Mother of the Church, pray for us. Mother of Divine Grace, pray for us. Mother most pure, pray for us. Mother most chaste, pray for us. Mother inviolate, pray for us. Mother undefiled, pray for us. Mother most amiable, pray for us. Mother most admirable, pray for us. Mother of good counsel, pray for us. Mother of our Creator, pray for us. Mother of our Savior, pray for us. | Mukama Maria omutukuvu, tusabire. Maama wa Katonda omutukuvu, tusabire. Omukyala omulongoosa omutukuvu, tusabire. Maama wa Kristo, tusabire. Maama w'Ekkanisa, tusabire. Maama w'ekisa kya Katonda, tusabire. Maama asinga obutukuvu, tusabire. Maama asinga obulongoosa, tusabire. Maama atalina kibi, tusabire. Maama asinga okwagalwa, tusabire. Maama asinga okusiimibwa, tusabire. Maama w'amagezi amalungi, tusabire. Maama w'Omutonzi waffe, tusabire. Maama w'Omulokozi waffe, tusabire. |
| Virgin most prudent, pray for us. Virgin most venerable, pray for us. Virgin most renowned, pray for us. Virgin most powerful, pray for us. Virgin most merciful, pray for us. Virgin most faithful, pray for us. | Mbeerera asinga obukugu, tusabire. Mbeerera asinga okuweebwa ekitiibwa, tusabire. Mbeerera asinga okumanyibwa, tusabire. Mbeerera asinga amaanyi, tusabire. Mbeerera asinga ekisa, tusabire. Mbeerera asinga obwesigwa, tusabire. |
| Mirror of justice, pray for us. Seat of wisdom, pray for us. Cause of our joy, pray for us. Spiritual vessel, pray for us. Vessel of honor, pray for us. Singular vessel of devotion, pray for us. Mystical rose, pray for us. Tower of David, pray for us. Tower of ivory, pray for us. House of gold, pray for us. Ark of the covenant, pray for us. Gate of heaven, pray for us. Morning star, pray for us. Health of the sick, pray for us. Refuge of sinners, pray for us. Comfort of the afflicted, pray for us. Help of Christians, pray for us. | Ekirabo ky'obwenkanya, tusabire. Ekifo eky'amagezi, tusabire. Ensibuko y'essanyu lyaffe, tusabire. Ekyuma ky'eby'omwoyo, tusabire. Ekyuma eky'ekitiibwa, tusabire. Ekyuma ekimu eky'okwewaayo, tusabire. Rose ey'amagero, tusabire. Omunaala gwa Dawudi, tusabire. Omunaala gw'amazina, tusabire. Ennyumba ya zaabu, tusabire. Essanduuko y'endagaano, tusabire. Oluggi lw'eggulu, tusabire. Emmunyeenye ey'enkya, tusabire. Obulamu bw'abalwadde, tusabire. Ekifo ky'abasajja aboonoonyi, tusabire. Okubudaabuda abali mu bulumi, tusabire. Obuyambi bw'Abakristaayo, tusabire. |
| Queen of Angels, pray for us. Queen of Patriarchs, pray for us. Queen of Prophets, pray for us. Queen of Apostles, pray for us. Queen of Martyrs, pray for us. Queen of Confessors, pray for us. Queen of Virgins, pray for us. Queen of all Saints, pray for us. Queen conceived without original sin, pray for us. Queen assumed into heaven, pray for us. Queen of the most holy Rosary, pray for us. Queen of families, pray for us. Queen of peace, pray for us. | Kwiini w'Abamalayika, tusabire. Kwiini w'Abakulembeze b'Eddiini, tusabire. Kwiini w'Abalaguzi, tusabire. Kwiini w'Abatume, tusabire. Kwiini w'Abajulizi, tusabire. Kwiini w'Abakkiriza, tusabire. Kwiini w'Abawala abatalina baana, tusabire. Kwiini w'Abatuukirivu bonna, tusabire. Kwiini eyazaalibwa nga talina kibi kyonna, tusabire. Kwiini eyatwalibwa mu ggulu, tusabire. Kwiini w'Essaala entukuvu ennyo, tusabire. Kwiini w'amaka, tusabire. Kwiini ow'emirembe, tusabire. |
| Lamb of God, who takes away the sins of the world, spare us, O Lord. Lamb of God, who takes away the sins of the world, graciously hear us, O Lord. Lamb of God, who takes away the sins of the world, have mercy on us. | Omwana gw'endiga owa Katonda, atwala ebibi by'ensi, otusonyiwe, Mukama waffe. Omwana gw'endiga owa Katonda, atwala ebibi by'ensi, owulire okusaba kwaffe mu kisa, Mukama waffe. Omwana gw'endiga owa Katonda, atwala ebibi by'ensi, twasaasira. |
| Pray for us, O Holy Mother of God. That we may be made worthy of the promises of Christ. | Tusabire, O Maama omutukuvu wa Katonda. Tuyambe okufuna ebyo Kristo bye yasuubiza. |
| Let us pray: Grant, we beseech you, O Lord God, that we your servants may enjoy perpetual health of mind and body; and by the intercession of the Blessed Mary, ever Virgin, may be delivered from present sorrow, and obtain eternal joy. Through Christ our Lord. Amen. | Ka tusabe: Tuwe, tukusaba, O Mukama Katonda, nti abaweereza bo basobole okufuna obulamu obutaggwaawo mu birowoozo n’omubiri; era nga tuyita mu kusabira kwa Mary eyatukuzibwa, omuwala atalina musango, tusobole okuva mu nnaku eziriwo kati, era tufune essanyu ery’olubeerera. Mu Kristo Mukama waffe. Amiina. |

| Near Calvary, Jesus falls once more, yet His love does not waver. | Aha rubaju rwa Calvary, Yesu akagwa omurundi ogundi, kwonka rukundo ye terahindukire. |
| Jesus is stripped of His garments | Yesu naajwaho ebijwaro bye |
| Jesus is humiliated, stripped of all dignity, for our redemption. | Yesu naahemuka, arikuhwaho ekitiinisa kyona, ahabwa okucungurwa kwaitu. |

| Opening Prayer | Okushaba kw'okwiguraho |
| All together pray: | Mweshengyereze hamwe: |
| You expired Jesus, But the source of life gushed forth for souls, and the ocean of mercy opened up for the whole world O Fount of Life, unfathomable Divine Mercy, envelop the whole world and empty Yourself out upon us. | Okahwaho Yesu, Kwonka oburugo bw'amagara bukaruga omu myoyo, kandi enyanja y'embabazi yaigura ahabw'ensi yoona O Fount of Life, embabazi za Ruhanga ezitarikwetegyerezibwa, shwekyera ensi yoona kandi oyeyihe aha itwe. |
| O Blood and Water | O Blood and Water |
| O Blood and Water which gushed forth from the Heart of Jesus as a fount of mercy for us, I trust in you! | O eshagama hamwe n'amaizi agashukire kuruga omu mutima gwa Yesu nk'ensibuko y'embabazi ahariitwe, ninkwikiriza! |
| Holy God, Holy Mighty One, Holy Immortal One, Have Mercy on us and on the whole world. (Say 3 times) Amen. | Ruhanga orikwera, Ow'amaani Orikwera, Orikutahwaho, Twine embabazi ahariitwe hamwe n'ensi yoona. (Gamba emirundi 3) Amen. |
| Jesus, King of Mercy, I trust in you! | Yesu, Omugabe w'embabazi, ninyesiga omuri iwe! |
| Our Father & Hail Mary | Taata waitu hamwe na Hail Mary |
| Leader: Our Father… All together: Give us this day… | Omwebembezi: Tataitwe… Boona hamwe: Otuhe ebyetaago byaitu ebya buriizooba |
| Leader: Hail Mary… All together: Holy Mary… | Omwebembezi: Hail Mary… Bona hamwe: Holy Mary… |
| The Apostles' Creed | Ekirayiro ky'abakwenda |
| All together pray the Creed: | Mwena hamwe mushabe ekiragiro: |
| I believe in God, the Father almighty, the creator of heaven and earth and in Jesus Christ, His only Son, Our Lord, who was conceived by the Holy Spirit, born of the Virgin Mary, suffered under Pontius Pilate, was crucified, died and was buried. He descended into hell. On the third day He rose again from the dead. He ascended into heaven and sits at the right hand of God, the Father almighty. From thence He shall come to judge the living and the dead. I believe in the Holy Spirit, the holy Catholic Church, the Communion of Saints, the forgiveness of sins, the resurrection of the body and life everlasting. Amen. | Ninyikiriza omuri Ruhanga, Ishe ow'obushoboorozi bwona, omuhangi w'eiguru n'ensi kandi omuri Yesu Kristo, Omwana we wenka, Mukama waitu, owaateekateekirwe omwoyo gurikwera, akazaarwa omukazi Mariya, akabonabona ahansi ya Pontius Pilate, akahanikwa aha musaraba, yaafa kandi yaaziikwa. Akagwa omu hell. Aha eizooba rya kashatu Akaimuka kuruga omu bafu. Akatemba omu iguru kandi ashutami aha mukono gwa buryo ogwa Ruhanga, Ishe ow'obushoboorozi bwona. Okuruga aho naija kwija kucwera abahuriire hamwe n'abafu orubanja. Ninyikiriza omu mwoyo gurikwera, Ekanisa y'Abakaturiki eyererezi, Okugaitwa kw'Abatukuvu, okusaasira ebibi, okuzooka kw'omubiri hamwe n'amagara agatahwaho. Amen. |
| On the Large Bead Before Each Decade | Aha Beedi Empango Buri Myaka Ikumi |
| All together: Eternal Father, I offer you the Body and Blood, Soul and Divinity of Your dearly beloved Son, Our Lord Jesus Christ in atonement for our sins and those of the whole world. | Byona hamwe: Taata ow'obutwire, ninkuhayo omubiri na eshagama, omwoyo hamwe n'obwa Ruhanga bw'Omwana waawe ou orikukunda munonga, Mukama waitu Yesu Kristo omu kushashurira ebibi byaitu hamwe n'eby'ensi yoona. |
| On the Ten Small Beads of Each Decade | Aha bukomooko ikumi obukye bwa buri mwaka |
| Leader: For the sake of His sorrowful passion All together: Have mercy on us and on the whole world. | Omwebembezi: Ahabw'okusaasira kw'omutima gwe Twena hamwe: Twagirire embabazi n'ensi yoona. |
| Concluding Prayer | Okushaba okw'okuhendera |
| Holy God, Holy Mighty One, Holy Immortal One, Have mercy on us and on the whole world. (Say three times) Amen. | Ruhanga orikwera, Omunyamaani orikwera, Orikutahwaho, Twine embabazi ahariitwe n'ensi yoona. (Gamba emirundi eshatu) Amen. |
| Jesus, King of Mercy, I trust in you! (say 3 times) | Yesu, Omugabe w'embabazi, ninyesiga omuri iwe! (gamba emirundi 3) |
| Pray these prayers once a day for 9 days | Shaba eshaara ezi omurundi gumwe buri izooba kumara ebiro 9 |
| How to Pray This Novena | Oku orikushaba Novena Egi |
| Pray the same prayers each day, for 9 days in a row. | Shaba eshaara emwe buri izooba, kumara ebiro 9 omu runyiriri. |
| Opening | Okwiguraho |
| In the Name of the Father, and of the Son, and of the Holy Spirit. Amen. | Omu iziina rya Ishe, n'erya Omwana, hamwe n'ery'Omwoyo Orikwera. Amiina. |
| Litany of Our Lady of Perpetual Help | Ekitabo ky'omukazi waitu ow'obuhwezi obutahwaho |
| Lord, have mercy on us. Christ, have mercy on us. | Mukama, tugirire embabazi. Kristo, tugirire embabazi. |
| Lord, have mercy on us. Christ, hear us. Christ, graciously hear us. | Mukama, twagirire embabazi. Kristo, tuhurikize. Kristo, mu muringo gw'embabazi tuhurikize. |
| God the Father of Heaven, Have mercy on us. | Ruhanga, Ishebo wa iguru, Twagirire embabazi. |
| God the Son, Redeemer of the world, Have mercy on us. | Ruhanga Omwana, omucunguzi w'ensi, Twine embabazi ahariitwe. |
| God the Holy Ghost, Have mercy on us. | Ruhanga Omwoyo Orikwera, Twagirire embabazi. |
| Holy Trinity, One God, Have mercy on us. | Omushaija, Omukazi n'omwana, Ruhanga omwe, Tugirire embabazi. |
| Holy Mary, pray for us. | Mariya orikwera, tushabire. |
| Holy Mother of God, pray for us. | Nyina Ruhanga orikwera, tushabire. |
| Holy Virgin of virgins, pray for us. | Omukazi orikwera w'abakazi abarikwera, tweshengyereze. |
| Mother of Christ, pray for us. | Maama wa Kristo, tushabire. |
| Queen conceived without the stain of Original Sin, pray for us. | Omugabekazi akatwara enda ataine kibi ky'okubanza, mutushabire. |
| Queen of the most Holy Rosary, pray for us. | Omugabekazi w'omusaraba ogurikwera munonga, tushabe. |
| Our Lady of Perpetual Help, pray for us. | Omukazi waitu ow'obuhwezi obutahwaho, tweshengyereze. |
| O Mother of Perpetual Help, whose very name inspires confidence, Come to my aid, O loving Mother. | O Maama w'obuhwezi obutahwaho, ow'eiziina rye eririkureeta obwesigye, Ija omu buhwezi bwangye, O Maama w'okukunda. |
| That I may love God with all my heart, Come to my aid, O loving Mother. | Kugira ngu nkunde Ruhanga n'omutima gwangye gwona, Ija onyambe, Nyina w'okukunda. |
| That I may in all things conform my will to that of thy Divine Son, Come to my aid, O loving Mother. | Nimbaasa omu bintu byona kuhikaana n'ekigyendererwa ky'omwana waawe ow'obwaruhanga, Ija onyambe, Nyina w'okukunda. |
| That I may always shun sin, the only real evil, Come to my aid, O loving Mother. | Nkabaasa kwanga ekibi, obubi bwonka obwabuzima, Oyambe, Nyina w'okukunda. |
| That I may always remember my last end, Come to my aid, O loving Mother. | Kugira ngu ninyijuka okuhendera kwangye okwahererukireyo, Ija omu kumpwera, O maama w'okukunda. |
| That I may often and devoutly receive the Sacraments, Come to my aid, O loving Mother. | Nimbaasa kuguma nintunga obujanjabi bw'okushaba, Mama w'omukundwa, nyamba. |
| That I may avoid every proximate occasion of sin, Come to my aid, O loving Mother. | Nimbaasa kwetantara buri kintu kyona ekirikubaasa kurugamu ekibi, Mukazi wangye orikundwa, nkwataho. |
| That I may never neglect prayer, Come to my aid, O loving Mother. | Kugira ngu ntakarekyera aho okushaba, Nkwija kukuhwera, O Maama w'okukunda. |
| That I may ever remember to invoke thee, particularly in time of temptation, Come to my aid, O loving Mother. | Nimbaasa kwijuka kushaba, okukira munonga omu bwire bw'okugyezibwa, Ija onyambe, Omuzaire w'omukundwa. |
| That I may always be victorious in the hour of temptation, Come to my aid, O loving Mother. | Kugira ngu ngyende omu maisho okusingura omu shaaha y'okwohibwa, Nkwataho, O Maama w'omukago. |
| That I may generously pardon my enemies, Come to my aid, O loving Mother. | Kugira ngu nshoboorore abazigu bangye, Ija omu kumpwera, O maama w'okukunda. |
| That I may arise quickly, should I have the misfortune of falling into mortal sin, Come to my aid, O loving Mother. | Nimbaasa kuguruka ahonaaho, Ku ndikuba nyine oburemeezi bw'okugwa omu kibi ky'okufa, Ija ompwere, O maama w'okukunda. |
| That I may courageously resist the seductions of evil companions, Come to my aid, O loving Mother. | Nkabaasa kugumisiriza okushendashenda kw'abanywani babi, Ija omu maisho gangye, Owaamaama w'okukunda. |
| That I may be strong against my own inconstancy, Come to my aid, O loving Mother. | Nimbaasa kuba ow'amaani omu butari bwesigwa bwangye, Oyambe, Nyina w'okukunda. |
| That I may not delay my conversion from day to day, Come to my aid, O loving Mother. | Kugira ngu ntarekye kuhindura okuruga aha izooba kuza aha rindi, Ija omu kumpwera, Nyina w'okukunda. |
| That I may labor zealously to eradicate my evil habits, Come to my aid, O loving Mother. | Nimbaasa kukora n'obweziriki kumaraho emicwe yangye mibi, Omwana w'omukazi owa rukundo, nyamba. |
| That I may ever love to serve thee, Come to my aid, O loving Mother. | Nimbaasa kukunda kukuheereza, Ija omu buhwezi bwangye, O maama w'okukunda. |
| That I may lead others to love and serve thee, Come to my aid, O loving Mother. | Kugira ngu ndebembe abandi kukunda n'okuheereza iwe, Ija omu kuhwera kwangye, Nyina w'okukunda. |
| That I may live and die in the friendship of God, Come to my aid, O loving Mother. | Nkabaasa kutuura kandi nkafa omu mukago gwa Ruhanga, Ija onyambe, Nyina w'okukunda. |
| In all necessities of body and soul, Come to my aid, O loving Mother. | Omu byetengo byona eby'omubiri hamwe n'omutima, Nkweshengyereza, nyine maama orikukunda. |
| In sickness and pain, Come to my aid, O loving Mother. | Omu burwaire n'obusaasi, Ija omu buhwezi bwangye, O Maama w'okukunda. |
| In poverty and distress, Come to my aid, O loving Mother. | Omu bworo n'obusaasi, Ija omu nyambaho, Nyina w'okukunda. |
| In persecution and abandonment, Come to my aid, O loving Mother. | Omu kubonabona n'okunagaijurwa, Ija omu bwire bwangye, O Maama w'omukago. |
| In grief and dereliction of mind, Come to my aid, O loving Mother. | Omu kushoberwa n'okuhwa amaani, Nkweshengyereza okunyamba, Nyina w'okukunda. |
| In time of war, famine and contagion, Come to my aid, O loving Mother. | Omu bwire bw'orutaro, enjara hamwe n'endwara, Ija omu buhwezi bwangye, O maama w'okukunda. |
| In every danger of sin, Come to my aid, O loving Mother. | Buri kabi koona akarikukwata aha kibi, Ija onyambe, Nyina w'okugonza. |
| When assailed by the evil spirits, Come to my aid, O loving Mother. | Ku ndaaba ninteerwa emyoyo emibi, Nkweshengyereza nyina wangye omukundwa. |
| When tempted by the allurements of a deceitful world, Come to my aid, O loving Mother. | Ku orikugyezibwa okukora ebintu by'ensi egi erimu oburyarya, Nkweshengyereza nyine maama w'okundinda. |
| When struggling against the inclinations of my corrupt nature, Come to my aid, O loving Mother. | Ku ndikurwanisa okwenda kw'omutima gwangye ogw'obushuma, Nkweshengyereza, Maama w'okukunda. |
| When tempted against the holy virtue of purity, Come to my aid, O loving Mother. | Ku orikugyezibwa aha kushemeza kw'oburungi bw'okwera, Ija omuyambe, Nyina wangye ow'okukunda. |
| When death is nigh, Come to my aid, O loving Mother. | Okufa ku kuraabe kuri haihi, Ija onyambe, nyina w'okukunda. |
| When the loss of my senses shall warn me that my earthly career is at an end, Come to my aid, O loving Mother. | Obu okufeerwa kw'obwengye bwangye kuraabe nikunyoreka ngu omurimo gwangye ogw'omu nsi guri aha muheru, Ija onyambe, O Maama w'okukunda. |
| When the thought of my approaching dissolution shall fill me with fear and terror, Come to my aid, O loving Mother. | Ku ndikuteekateeka aha kushenyuka kwangye kuriija kunshemeza n'obutiini hamwe n'okutiina, Ija omu maisho gangye, O Maama w'omukundwa. |
| When at the decisive hour of death, the evil spirit will endeavor to plunge my soul into despair, Come to my aid, O loving Mother. | Ku ndikuba ndi omu shaaha y'okufa, omwoyo mubi nigwija kugyezaho kutuma omwoyo gwangye guhwa amaani, Ija onyambe, Nyina wangye orikukunda. |
| When the priest of God shall give me his last absolution and his last blessing, Come to my aid, O loving Mother. | Omubuurizi wa Ruhanga ku araaheze okunyihaho ebibi bye n'omugisha gwe, Ija omumpwere obuhwezi, Nyina wangye ow'okukunda. |
| When my friends and relations, surrounding my bed, moved with compassion, shall invoke thy clemency on my behalf, Come to my aid, O loving Mother. | Abanywani bangye hamwe n'abanywani, barikwetoorora ekitanda kyangye, baakoresa embabazi, babaase kukushaba okunshazira, Oyambe, Nyina w'okukunda. |
| When the world will vanish from my sight, and my heart will cease to beat, Come to my aid, O loving Mother. | Obu ensi eraija kuhwaho omu maisho gangye, kandi omutima gwangye guhwe kuteera, Ija okunyambere, Owaanyinabukundwa. |
| When I shall yield my soul into the hands of its Creator, Come to my aid, O loving Mother. | Ku ndaheereze omwoyo gwangye omu mikono y'Omuhangi waagwo, Ija okunyamba, Nyina wangye ow'okukunda. |
| When my soul will appear before its Sovereign Judge, Come to my aid, O loving Mother. | Omwoyo gwangye ku guraareebekye omu maisho ga Omucwi w'Emishango, Ija onyambe, Nyina w'okukunda. |
| When the irrevocable sentence will be pronounced, Come to my aid, O loving Mother. | Ku orikwija kugamba ekigambo ky'okusharaho, Ija omu mbabazi zangye, Nyina w'omukundwa. |
| When I will be suffering in Purgatory, and sighing for the vision of God, Come to my aid, O loving Mother. | Ku ndaabe ninyija kuba nintuntuura omu purigatori, kandi ndikweteekateekyera okureeba Ruhanga, Ija onnyambe, Owaanyinaze ow'okukunda. |
| Lamb of God, Who takest away the sins of the world, Spare us, O Lord. | Omwana gw'entaama, orikwiha ebibi by'ensi, Twikirize, Mukama. |
| Lamb of God, Who takest away the sins of the world, Graciously hear us, O Lord. | Omwana gw'entaama, orikwihaho ebibi by'ensi, Mukama w'embabazi, tuhurire. |
| Lamb of God, Who takest away the sins of the world, Have mercy on us. | Omwana wa Ruhanga, orikwihaho ebibi by'ensi, Tugirire embabazi. |
| Let Us Pray | Ka tushabe |
| O Almighty and merciful God, Who, in order to succor the human race, hast willed the Blessed Virgin Mary to become the Mother of Thy only-begotten Son, grant, we beseech Thee, that by her intercession we may avoid the contagion of sin and serve Thee with a pure heart, through the same Christ Our Lord. | Owa Ruhanga ow'amaani kandi ow'embabazi, Orikwenda kuhwera abantu, okasharamu omukazi w'omugisha Mariya kuba Maama w'Omwana waawe omwe wenka, tuheereze, nitushaba ngu kurabira omu kushaba kwe, twetantare ekibi kandi tukuheereze n'omutima ogurikwera, kurabira omuri Kristo Mukama waitu. |
| Amen. | Amen. |
| Prayer to Our Lady of Perpetual Help | Okushaba kwaitu omukazi w'obuhwezi obutahwaho |
| O Mother of Perpetual Help, grant that I may ever invoke your powerful name, the protection of the living and the salvation of the dying. | O Maama w'obuhwezi obutahwaho, mpereza ngu nshemereire kushaba eiziina ryawe ery'amaani, okurinda abahuriire hamwe n'okukiza abarikufa. |
| Purest Mary, let your name henceforth be ever on my lips. Delay not, Blessed Lady, to rescue me whenever I call on you. | Purest Mary, reka eiziina ryawe kuruga hati rishemereire kuba aha mimwa yangye. Otakyerererwa, Omukazi Orikwera, okunkiza buri murundi ogu ndikukushaba. |
| In my temptations, in my needs, I will never cease to call on you, ever repeating your sacred name, Mary, Mary. | Omu kwohibwa kwangye, omu byetengo byangye, tindikwija kuhwa kukushaba, ndikugarukamu eiziina ryawe ery'ekitiinisa, Mariya, Mariya. |
| What a consolation, what sweetness, what confidence fills my soul when I utter your sacred name or even only think of you! I thank the Lord for having given you so sweet, so powerful, so lovely a name. | N'eky'okuhuumuriza, n'oburungi, n'obwesigye oburikwijura omu mutima gwangye ku ndikugamba eiziina ryawe eririkwera nainga n'obu ndaabe ninteekateeka ahariiwe! Ninsiima Mukama ahabw'okukuha eiziina rirungi munonga, ery'amaani, kandi eririkushemeza. |
| But I will not be content with merely uttering your name. Let my love for you prompt me ever to hail you Mother of Perpetual Help. Mother of Perpetual Help, pray for me and grant me the favor I confidently ask of you | Kwonka tindikwija kumarwa n'okugamba eiziina ryawe. Ka rukundo yangye ahabwawe entebekanise okukusiima iwe Maama w'Obuhwezi Obutahwaho. Maama w'Obuhwezi Obutahwaho, nsabira kandi ompe oburungi obu ndikushaba kuruga ahariiwe |
| (mention request here…) | (orekure okushaba aha…) |
| Hail Mary (three times) | Hail Mary (emirundi eshatu) |
| Closing | Okukinga |
| From Good Friday to the Saturday before Divine Mercy Sunday | Okuruga aha kyakataano kirungi kuhisya aha Kyamukaaga ekyabaire kiriho omu kiro ky'embabazi z'omuhangi |
| Pray these prayers each day, for 9 days in a row, followed by the Divine Mercy Chaplet. | Shaba okushaba oku buri izooba, kumara ebiro 9 byona, orikukuratirwa Chapleti y'embabazi za Ruhanga. |
| The Words of Jesus | Ebigambo bya Yesu |
| “Today bring to Me all mankind, especially all sinners, and immerse them in the ocean of My mercy. In this way you will console Me in the bitter grief into which the loss of souls plunges Me.” | "Erizooba ntwara abantu boona, okukira munonga abasiisi boona, kandi obate omu nyanja y'embabazi zangye. Omu muringo ogu noija kunyihaho obusaasi bw'okufeerwa amagara." |
| Prayer | Okushaba |
| Most Merciful Jesus, whose very nature it is to have compassion on us and to forgive us, do not look upon our sins but upon our trust which we place in Your infinite goodness. Receive us all into the abode of Your Most Compassionate Heart, and never let us escape from It. We beg this of You by Your love which unites You to the Father and the Holy Spirit. | Yesu Omusaasizi, ow'obuhangwa bwe kubaasa kutugirira embabazi n'okutusasira, otakareeba ebibi byaitu kwonka kureka okureeba okwesiga kwaitu oku turikuta omu burungi bwawe obutarikuhwaho. Twakiire twena omu mwanya gw'omutima gwawe ogurikukirayo kugirira abantu embabazi, kandi otatwikiriza kuruga omuri Gwo. Nitushaba eki ahari iwe ahabw'okukunda kwawe okurikukwatanisa naiwe na Ishe hamwe n'Omwoyo Orikwera. |
| Eternal Father, turn Your merciful gaze upon all mankind and especially upon poor sinners, all enfolded in the Most Compassionate Heart of Jesus. For the sake of His sorrowful Passion show us Your mercy, that we may praise the omnipotence of Your mercy for ever and ever. Amen. | Taata ow'obutwire, hindura amaisho gaawe ag'embabazi aha bantu boona kandi okukira munonga aha nshobe abooro, boona barikushwekwa omutima gwa Yesu ogurikusaasira. Ahabw'okubonabona kwe okw'obusaasi, tworekye embabazi zaawe, kugira ngu twebaze obushoboorozi bw'embabazi zaawe ebiro byona na ebiro byona. Amiina. |
| The Divine Mercy Chaplet | Ekisabirizi ky'embabazi za Ruhanga |
| Pray the Divine Mercy Chaplet. You can use ordinary Rosary beads. | Shaba omushana gwa Divine Mercy Chaplet. Noobaasa kukoresa obukomo bwa Rosary obwa buriijo. |
| 1. Make the Sign of the Cross. | 1. Kora akamanyiso k'omusharaba. |
| 2. Pray the optional opening prayers (below). | 2. Shaba okushaba kw'okwiguraho okw'omutaano (ahansi). |
| 3. On the first beads above the crucifix, pray one Our Father, one Hail Mary and the Apostles’ Creed. | 3. Aha beedi z'okubanza eziri ahaiguru y'omusaaraba, shaba Our Father emwe, Hail Mary emwe hamwe n'ekiragiro ky'abakwenda. |
| 4. On each Our Father bead, say: Eternal Father, I offer Thee the Body and Blood, Soul and Divinity of Thy dearly beloved Son, Our Lord Jesus Christ, in atonement for our sins and those of the whole world. | 4. Aha buri bead ya Our Father, gamba: Taata ow'obutwire, ninkuhayo Omubiri hamwe n'eshagama, Omwoyo hamwe n'Oburungi bw'Omwana waawe ou orikukunda munonga, Mukama waitu Yesu Kristo, omu kushashura ebibi byaitu hamwe n'eby'ensi yoona. |
| 5. On each of the ten Hail Mary beads, say: For the sake of His sorrowful Passion, have mercy on us and on the whole world. | 5. Aha buri kamwe aha mpehu ikumi za Hail Mary, gamba oti: Ahabw'okusaasira kwe okw'obusaasi bw'omutima, tugirire embabazi hamwe n'ensi yoona. |
| 6. Repeat steps 4 and 5 for all five decades. | 6. Garukamu emitendera ya 4 na 5 ahabw'emyaka makumi ataano yoona. |
| 7. Conclude with the Holy God prayer, three times: Holy God, Holy Mighty One, Holy Immortal One, have mercy on us and on the whole world. | 7. Okuhendera n'okushaba Ruhanga orikwera, emirundi eshatu: Ruhanga orikwera, Omunyamagyezi orikwera, Omuntu otarikufa, twegire embabazi hamwe n'ensi yoona. |
| 8. Pray the optional closing prayers (below). | 8. Shaba okushaba kw'okuhendera okw'okwetenga (ahansi). |
| Optional Opening Prayers | Okushaba kw'okwiguraho okw'okwetegyeka |
| St. Faustina’s Prayer for Sinners | Okushaba kwa St. Faustina ahabw'abasiisi |
| O Jesus, eternal Truth, our Life, I call upon You and I beg Your mercy for poor sinners. O sweetest Heart of my Lord, full of pity and unfathomable mercy, I plead with You for poor sinners. O Most Sacred Heart, Fount of Mercy from which gush forth rays of inconceivable graces upon the entire human race, I beg of You light for poor sinners. O Jesus, be mindful of Your own bitter Passion and do not permit the loss of souls redeemed at so dear a price of Your most precious Blood. O Jesus, when I consider the great price of Your Blood, I rejoice at its immensity, for one drop alone would have been enough for the salvation of all sinners. Although sin is an abyss of wickedness and ingratitude, the price paid for us can never be equaled. Therefore, let every soul trust in the Passion of the Lord, and place its hope in His mercy. God will not deny His mercy to anyone. Heaven and earth may change, but God’s mercy will never be exhausted. Oh, what immense joy burns in my heart when I contemplate Your incomprehensible goodness, O Jesus! I desire to bring all sinners to Your feet that they may glorify Your mercy throughout endless ages (Diary of Saint Maria Faustina Kowalska, 72). | O Yesu, Amazima agatarikuhwaho, Amagara gaitu, ninkushaba kandi ninkushaba okusaasira kwawe ahabw'abasiisi abooro. O omutima ogurikukirayo oburungi ogwa Mukama wangye, ogwijwire okusaasira hamwe n'okusaasira okutarikubaasa kugambwaho, ninkushaba ahabw'abasiisi abooro. O omutima ogurikukirayo oburungi, Oburugo bw'Okusaasira obu orutambi rw'embabazi ezitarikubaasa kuteekateekwaho rurikuruga omuri zo aha bantu boona, ninkushaba omushana gw'abasiisi abooro. O Yesu, teekateeka aha kuhurira kwawe okw'obusaasi kandi otakikiriza okufeerwa emyoyo eyacungwirwe aha mihendo y'omuhendo gw'eshagama yaawe erikukirayo oburungi. O Yesu, ku ndikureeba omuhendo gw'eshagama yaawe, nshemererwa ahabw'obuhango bwayo, ahabw'okuba akatondo kamwe kokaba nikamara okucungura abasiisi boona. N'obu ekibi kiraabe kiri ekiina ky'obubi hamwe n'obutasiima, omuhendo ogurashashurwa ahabwaitu tigurikubaasa kugyeragyeranisibwa. N'ahabw'ekyo, reka buri myoyo yeesigye omu kuhurira kw'obusaasi kwa Mukama, kandi ete amatsiko gaayo omu kusaasira kwe. Ruhanga tarikwija kwanga kusaasira kwe ahari weena. Iguru hamwe n'ensi nibibaasa kuhinduka, kwonka okusaasira kwa Ruhanga tikurikwija kuhwaho. Oh, n'okushemererwa kw'amaani okurikwotsya omu mutima gwangye ku ndikureeba oburungi bwawe obutarikwetegyerezibwa, O Yesu! Ninyenda kureeta abasiisi boona aha bigyere byawe kugira ngu bahimbise okusaasira kwawe omu myaka etarikuhwaho (Diary of Saint Maria Faustina Kowalska, 72). |
| You expired, Jesus, but the source of life gushed forth for souls, and the ocean of mercy opened up for the whole world. O Fount of Life, unfathomable Divine Mercy, envelop the whole world and empty Yourself out upon us. | Okahwa amaani, Yesu, kwonka oburugo bw'amagara bukarugamu ahabw'emyoyo, kandi enyanja y'embabazi yaigura ahabw'ensi yoona. O Fount of Life, embabazi z'obuhangwa ezitarikwetegyerezibwa, shwekyera ensi yoona kandi oyeyongyere aha itwe. |
| O Blood and Water, which gushed forth from the Heart of Jesus as a fount of mercy for us, I trust in You! (three times) | O Blood and Water, eyashukire kuruga omu mutima gwa Yesu nk'ekyambu ky'embabazi ahabwaitu, ninyesiga omuri iwe! (emirundi eshatu) |
| The Apostles’ Creed | Ekihamya ky'Abatume |
| I believe in God, the Father Almighty, Creator of heaven and earth, and in Jesus Christ, his only Son, our Lord, who was conceived by the power of the Holy Spirit, born of the Virgin Mary, suffered under Pontius Pilate, was crucified, died and was buried; he descended to the dead; on the third day he rose again from the dead; he ascended into Heaven, and is seated at the right hand of God the Father Almighty; from there he will come to judge the living and the dead. | Ninyikiriza omuri Ruhanga, Isheboona ow'amaani goona, Omutonzi w'eiguru n'ensi, kandi na Yesu Kristo, Omwana we wenka, Mukama waitu, owaateekateekirwe omu maani g'Omwoyo Orikwera, akazaarwa Omukazi Mariamu, yabonabona ahansi ya Pontius Pilate, yakomererwa aha musaraba, yafa kandi yaaziikwa; akashuuma omu bafu; aha eizooba rya kashatu akagaruka yaazooka kuruga omu bafu; akatemba omu iguru, kandi ashutami aha mukono gwa buryo gwa Ruhanga Isheboona ow'amaani goona; kuruga aho naija kwija kucwera abahuriire hamwe n'abafiire. |
| I believe in the Holy Spirit, the holy Catholic Church, the Communion of Saints, the forgiveness of sins, the resurrection of the body, and life everlasting. | Ninyikiriza omu Mwoyo Orikwera, ekanisa y'abakaturiki eyerikwera, okukwatanisa kw'abarikwera, okusaasirwa ebibi, okuzooka kw'omubiri, n'amagara agatarikuhwaho. |
| Optional Closing Prayers | Okushaba kw'okukinga okw'okwetooranira |
| Eternal God, in whom mercy is endless and the treasury of compassion, inexhaustible, look kindly upon us and increase Your mercy in us, that in difficult moments we might not despair nor become despondent, but with great confidence submit ourselves to Your holy will, which is Love and Mercy itself. | Ruhanga ow'obutwire, ou embabazi ze zitarikuhwa kandi n'ekitabo ky'embabazi, ekitarikuhwa, tureebe omu muringo murungi kandi oyongyere embabazi zaawe omuriitwe, ngu omu bwire bw'oburemeezi tutahwa amaani nainga okuhwa amaani, kwonka n'obwesigye bwingi twehaireyo aha kigyendererwa Kyawe ekirikwera, ekiri rukundo hamwe n'embabazi zonka. |
| O Greatly Merciful God, Infinite Goodness, today all mankind calls out from the abyss of its misery to Your mercy, to Your compassion, O God; and it is with its mighty voice of misery that it cries out. Gracious God, do not reject the prayer of this earth’s exiles! O Lord, Goodness beyond our understanding, Who are acquainted with our misery through and through, and know that by our own power we cannot ascend to You, we implore You: anticipate us with Your grace and keep on increasing Your mercy in us, that we may faithfully do Your holy will all through our life and at death’s hour. Let the omnipotence of Your mercy shield us from the darts of our salvation’s enemies, that we may with confidence, as Your children, await Your [Son’s] final coming, that day known to You alone. And we expect to obtain everything promised us by Jesus in spite of all our wretchedness. For Jesus is our Hope: through His merciful Heart, as through an open gate, we pass through to heaven (Diary, 1570). | Ruhanga ow'embabazi nyingi, oburungi obutarikuhwaho, eriizooba abantu boona nibashaba kuruga omu kabi kaabo kuhika aha mbabazi zaawe, n'okusaasira kwaawe, Ruhanga; kandi nibashaba n'eiraka ryabo ery'amaani ery'obusaasi. Ruhanga w'embabazi, otakanga okushaba kw'abantu abaabingirwe omu nsi! O Mukama, Oburungi obutarikwetegyerezibwa, orikumanya obusaasi bwaitu kurungi, kandi orikumanya ngu omu maani gaitu titurikubaasa kugyenda ahariiwe, nitukushaba: otuhe embabazi zaawe kandi ogumizemu okwongyera embabazi zaawe omuriitwe, kugira ngu tukore ebyo ebirikwera byawe omu bwesigwa omu magara gaitu goona hamwe n'omu shaaha y'okufa. Reka amaani ga embabazi zaawe gatuboneze kuruga omu mizingo y'abazigu b'okucungurwa kwaitu, kugira ngu tubaase kutegyereza okwiija kwa [Omwana waawe] okwahamuheru, ekiro ekirikumanywa naiwe wenka. Kandi nituteekateeka kutunga buri kimwe eki Yesu yaaraganiise n'obu twakuba turi ababi munonga. Ahabwokuba Yesu niwe amatsiko gaitu: kurabira omu mutima gwe gw'embabazi, nk'oku turikuraba omu rwigi orwigwireho, nituraba omu iguru (Diary, 1570). |
| Prayer to St. Jude | Okushaba kwa St. Jude |
| Most holy Apostle, St. Jude, faithful servant and friend of Jesus, the Church honors and invokes you universally, as the patron of difficult cases, of things almost despaired of. | Omukwenda orikukirayo oburungi, St. Jude, omuheereza w'obwesigwa kandi munywani wa Yesu, Ekanisa neekuhaisa ekitiinisa kandi neekushaba omunsi yoona, nk'omukuumi w'emishango egumire, y'ebintu ebirikuba biri haihi kuhwa amaani. |
| Pray for me, as I feel helpless and alone. | Nshaba onshabire, ahabwokuba ndi omu kushoberwa kandi nyine enaku. |
| Intercede with God for me that He bring visible and speedy help where help is almost despaired of. Come to my assistance in this great need that I may receive the consolation and help of heaven in all my necessities, tribulations, and sufferings, particularly: | Nshaba Ruhanga ngu akundeere obuhwezi bw'amaani kandi bw'ahonaaho ahu obuhwezi burikuba buri haihi kuhwa amaani. Ija omu buhwezi bwangye omu bwetago obu bukuru kugira ngu ntungye okuhuumurizibwa n'obuhwezi bw'omu iguru omu byetengo byangye byona, okubonabona kwangye, hamwe n'okubonabona kwangye, namunonga: |
| and that I may praise God with you and all the saints forever. I promise, O Blessed St. Jude, to be ever mindful of this great favor granted me by God and to always honor you as my special and powerful patron, and to gratefully encourage devotion to you. | kandi ngu nshemereire kuhimbisa Ruhanga hamwe naiwe hamwe na abarikwera boona ebiro byona. Nindaganisa, O Blessed St. Jude, kuguma ninteekateeka aha mugisha gw'amaani ogu Ruhanga yaampa kandi okuheereza obusingye bwawe nk'omukuumi wangye ow'omutaano kandi ow'amaani, kandi okuhiga okumwesigwa omu muringo gw'okusiima. |
| Praise to the Sacred Heart | Okusiima omutima ogurikwera |
| May the Most Sacred Heart of Jesus be adored, and loved in all the tabernacles until the end of time. Amen. | Omwoyo gwa Yesu ogurikwera munonga guheebwe ekitiinisa, kandi gukundwe omu mizigiti yoona kuhisya aha muheru gw'obwire. Amiina. |
| May the most Sacred Heart of Jesus be praised and glorified now and forever. Amen. | Omutima gwa Yesu ogurikwera munonga guhimbisibwe kandi gutunguurwe hati n'ebiro byona. Amiina |
| St. Jude pray for me and hear my prayers. Amen. | St. Jude ansabire kandi ahurikize okushaba kwangye. Amen. |
| Blessings | Emigisha |
| Blessed be the Sacred Heart of Jesus. | Ahabwe okukunda kw'omutima gwa Yesu. |
| Blessed be the Immaculate Heart of Mary. | Ekitinisa kibe aha mutima gwa Mariya ogutaine kamogo. |
| Blessed be St. Jude Thaddeus, in all the world and for all Eternity. | Ahabwe St. Jude Thaddeus, omu nsi yoona hamwe na obutwire bwona. |
| Concluding Prayers | Okushaba okw'okuhendera |
| Our Father Hail Mary Glory Be | Taata waitu Hail Mary Ekitiinisa kyaba |
| Jesus and His 12 Apostles, over 13 consecutive days | Yesu hamwe n'entumwa ze 12, kumara ebiro 13 by'okukuratana |
| Opening Prayer (Read Twice) | Okushaba kw'okwiguraho (shoma emirundi ebiri) |
| Oh my 13 Blessed souls so wise and understanding, I ask you for the Love of God that my request be answered. | Oh my 13 Blessed souls abaine obwengye kandi abarikwetegyereza, nimbashaba okukunda kwa Ruhanga ngu okushaba kwangye kugarukwe. |
| Of you I ask for the sake of the blood that Jesus shed that my request be answered. | Nimbashaba ahabw'eshagama ya Yesu eyaasheeshe, okushaba kwangye kugarukwe. |
| Prayer to Our Lord | Okushaba kwa Mukama waitu |
| My Lord Jesus Christ that your protection wrap me with your arms. Guard me with your eyes. O God of kindness you have been my defender in life and death. I ask that you free me from the difficulties that torment me. | Mukama wangye Yesu Kristo ngu oburinzi bwawe bumboneze n'emikono yaawe. Nkureeberere n'amaisho gaawe. O Ruhanga w'embabazi ogumire ori omurwanirizi wangye omu magara n'omu kufa. Ninkushaba ngu onshoboorore kuruga omu buremeezi oburikunyigiriza. |
| Closing Prayer | Okushaba kw'okuhendera |
| My 13 blessed souls so wise and understanding having received the grace I seek from you (state your request) I will be devoted to you. | Emyoyo yangye 13 eyaheebwa omugisha, eri obwengye kandi erikwetegyereza, etungire embabazi ei ndikuronda kuruga ahariiwe (gamba eki orikushaba) Ninyija kuba ninkuheereza. |
| Instruction | Ekiragiro |
| Say 13 Our Father's and 13 Hail Mary's for 13 consecutive days. | Gamba Our Father 13 na Hail Mary 13 aha biro 13 ebirikukurataho. |
| Prayer to Pope St. Leo the Great | Okushaba kwa Paapa St. Leo ow'ahaiguru |
| Glory be to thee, O Jesus, Lion of the Tribe of Judah! that hast raised up in thy Church a Lion to defend her in those dark times, when holy Faith was most exposed to danger. | Ekitiinisa kibe ahariiwe, O Yesu, Entare y'oruganda rwa Yuda! owaayimukize omu kanisa yaawe entare y'okurinda omu bwire obwo obw'omwirima, obu okwikiriza okurikwera kwabaire kuri omu kabi kahango. |
| Thou charged Peter with confirming his Brethren (St. Luke, xxii. 32.); and we have seen Leo, in whom Peter lived, fulfill this office with sovereign authority. We have heard the acclamation of the holy Council, which, in admiration at the heavenly teachings of Leo, proclaimed the signal favour thou conferred on thy Flock, when thou commanded Peter to feed both Sheep and Lambs. | Okashaba Peter okuhamya ab'eishe - emwe be (St. Luke, xxii. 32.); kandi twareeba Leo, ou Peter yaabaire arimu, arikuhikiiriza omurimo ogu n'obushoborozi bw'amaani. Twahuriire okwakiira kw'orukiiko orurikwera, orwabaire nirwenda enyegyesa za Leo ez'omu iguru, rwarangirira obuheereza bw'omutaano obu waataire aha b'entaama zaawe, obu waaragiira Peter kuriisa embuzi hamwe n'embuzi. |
| O holy Pontiff Leo! thou worthily represented Peter in his Chair, whence thy apostolic teaching ceased not to flow, ever beautiful in its truth and majesty. The Church of thine own day honored thee as the great Teacher of Faith; and the Church of every succeeding age has recognized thee as one of the most learned Doctors and preachers of the divine Word. | O holy Pontiff Leo! okaba nookuratira Peter omu ntebe ye, ahu enyegyesa yaawe ey'obutumwa etararekire kugyenda, erikworeka oburungi bw'amazima hamwe n'obukuru bwayo. Ekanisa y'omurembe gwawe ekakuhaisa ekitiinisa nk'Omwegyesa mukuru w'Eby'okwikiriza; kandi Ekanisa y'emyaka yoona eyakuratsireho ekakumanya nk'omwe aha bashomi abarikukirayo obwengye hamwe n'ababuurizi b'Ekigambo kya Ruhanga. |
| From thy throne in heaven, where now thou reign, pour forth upon us the understanding of the great Mystery which thou was called on to defend. Under thy inspired pen, this mystery grows clear; we see how sublimely it harmonizes with all other mysteries; and Faith delights at gaining so close a view of the divine object of its belief. Oh! strengthen this Faith within us. | Okuruga aha ntebe yaawe omu iguru, ahu hati orikugira obutegyeki, tuhe okwetegyereza kw'ekihama ky'amaani eki waayetwa kurinda. Ahansi y'ekyoma kyawe ekyahandiikirwe, ekihama eki nikyeyongyera kuba kirungi; nitureeba oku kirikuhikaana kurungi n'ebihama ebindi byona; kandi Okwikiriza nikushemererwa ahabw'okutunga okureeba okwahamire aha kigyendererwa ky'obuhangwa bw'okwikiriza kwakyo. Oh! Guma amaani g'Okwikiriza oku omuriitwe. |
| The Incarnate Word is blasphemed in these our own times; avenge his glory, by sending us men of thy zeal and learning. | Ekigambo ekirikutukura nikijumwa omu bwire obu; okuhayo ekitiinisa kye, oratwohereza abashaija b'okwehayo n'obwegyese. |
| For Our Times | Omu bwire bwaitu |
| Thou triumphed over barbarian invaders: Attila acknowledged the influence of thy sanctity and eloquence, by withdrawing his troops from the Christian land they infested. | Okasingura abataahi b'obutagira kitiinisa: Attila akamanya amaani g'oburungi bwawe hamwe n'okugamba kwawe, obwo arikwihaho amahe ge kuruga omu mahanga g'Abakristaayo agu yaabaire atwiremu. |
| In these our days, there have risen up new barbarians, civilized barbarians, who would persuade us that religion should be eliminated from Education, and that the State, in its laws and institutions, should simply ignore our Lord Jesus Christ, the King to whom all power has been given, not only in heaven but on Earth also (St. Matth. xxviii. 18). | Omu biro ebi, habaireho abantu basya abarikwetwa abarikukora ebikorwa by'obushaarizi, abarikwetwa abarikukora ebikorwa by'obushaarizi kandi abarikwetegyerezibwa, abarikubaasa kutuhiga ngu ediini eshemereire kubingwa omu by'obwegyese, kandi ngu Gavumenti, omu biragiro byayo hamwe n'ebitongore byayo, eshemereire kwetantara Mukama waitu Yesu Kristo, Omugabe ou amaani goona gahairwe, ti omu iguru honka, kureka n'omu nsi (St. Matth. xxviii. 18). |
| Oh! help us by thy powerful intercession, for our danger is extreme. Many are seduced, and are apostates whilst flattering themselves that they are still Christians. | Oh! otuhwere kurabira omu kushaba kwawe okw'amaani, ahabwokuba akabi kaitu kari kakye. Abantu baingi nibabeihabeiha, kandi nibeija kuba abahakani b'ediini barikwehimbisa ngu bakiri Abakristaayo. |
| Pray that the light, that is left within us, may not be extinguished, and that the public scandals, which now exist, may be brought to an end. Attila was but a pagan; our modern statesmen and Governments are, or, at least, call themselves, Christians: have pity on them, and gain for them light to see the precipice to which they are hurrying society. | Shaba ngu omushana, ogurikuguma omuriitwe, gutahwaho, kandi ngu ebishobobo by'abantu boona, ebiriho hati, bibe nibiija kuhendera. Attila akaba ari omupagani; abeebembezi b'amahanga gaitu hamwe na Gavumenti zaitu eza hati nibo, nainga, haakiri, beeyeta Abakristaayo: bagirire embabazi, kandi obahe omushana gw'okureeba ekiina eki barikuhindura abantu. |
| For the Faithful and the Church | Ahabw'abesigwa hamwe n'ekanisa |
| These days of Paschal Time must remind thee, O holy Pontiff! of the Easters thou didst once spend, here on earth, when, surrounded by the Neophytes, thou gave them the nourishment of thy magnificent Discourses: pray for the Faithful, who have this Easter, risen to a new life with Christ. | Ebiro ebi by'obwire bwa Paschal, nibiteekwa kukwijutsya, iwe Omutakatifu Pontiff! aha bwire obu waamazire omu burugwa izooba, aha nsi, obu waabaire oyetoroirwe abarikwetwa Neophytes, okabaheereza ebyokurya by'okubagambira kwawe okurungi: shaba ahabw'Abikiriza, abaine eki Easter, abazookire omu magara masya na Kristo. |
| What they most stand in need of is, a fuller and better knowledge of this their Saviour, in order that they may cling more closely to him, and persevere in his holy service. Thy prayers must get them this knowledge; by thy prayers, thou must teach them what he is both in his Divine and Human Nature: that, as God, He is their Last End, and their Judge after death; as Man, their Brother, their Redeemer, their Model. | Eki barikukirayo kwetenga n'okumanya kurungi kandi okurungi okw'omujuni waabo, kugira ngu bagume bamukwatanise munonga, kandi bagumizemu omu buheereza bwe oburikwera. Okushaba kwawe kushemereire kubaha okumanya oku; kurabira omu kushaba kwawe, oshemereire kubegyesa eki ari omu muringo gwe ogwa Ruhanga hamwe n'ogw'obuntu: ngu, nk'oku ari Ruhanga, niwe muheru gwabo, kandi omucwi w'emishango bwanyima y'okufa; nk'omuntu, niwe murumuna waabo, omucunguzi waabo, hamwe n'eky'okureeberaho kyabo. |
| Bless, O Leo! and help the Pontiff who is now thy successor on the Chair of Peter. Show now thy love for that Rome, whose sacred and eternal destinies were so frequently the subject of thy glowing and heavenly eloquence. | Bless, O Leo! kandi oyambe Pontiff orikukora hati nk'omusigire waawe aha ntebe ya Peter. Yoreka obwahati rukundo yaawe aha Rome, ei eby'obuhangwa byayo ebirikwera kandi eby'obutwire bikaba biri eby'okugambwaho omu bigambo byawe ebirikwaka kandi eby'omu iguru. |
| Intention | Ekigyendererwa |
| We also ask, St Leo, that you pray for us in our intention (mention request here…) | Kandi nitushaba, St Leo, ngu otushabire omu kigyendererwa kyaitu (yoreka okushaba aha…) |
| Pope St Leo the Great, pray for us! | Papa St Leo omukuru, tushabire! |
| Prayer to Our Lady of Guadalupe | Okushaba kwa Mukama waitu wa Guadalupe |
| Remember, O most gracious Virgin of Guadalupe, that in your heavenly apparitions on the mount of Tepeyac, you promised to show your compassion and pity toward all who, loving and trusting you, seek your help and call upon you in their necessities and afflictions. | Ijuka, O omukazi w'ekitiinisa kya Virgin wa Guadalupe, ngu omu kureebeka kwawe okw'omu iguru aha rushozi rwa Tepeyac, okaraganisa kworeka embabazi n'okusaasira abo boona, barikukunda kandi barikukwesiga, barikuronda obuhwezi bwawe kandi barikukushaba omu byetaago byabo hamwe n'obusaasi. |
